Could use direct low-dimensional continuous optimization at high resolution to find better shape. Let's design a general pipeline:
1. Initialize with several shapes (parent, power ramps, linear/quadratic, B-spline).
2. Optimize shape at base n (maybe 2048) using a combination of:
   - Smooth log-sum-exp objective with mirror descent / gradient with line search and adaptive temperature β.
   - Exact coordinate pair moves (move mass i->j) with convex minimizer, using top peaks and gradient.
   - LP/Frank-Wolfe updates if feasible (scipy/cvxpy) at reduced length to diversify.
   - Small-n global DE/basin-hopping to propose divergent shapes.
3. Multi-scale: optimize at n0 (e.g., 256/512), upscale to 1024, 2048, 4096/4200, refine. Or optimize directly at 4200 using parent.
4. Return best.

Golden search for convex:
```
gr = 0.618
a=0; b=tmax
if b<=0: return None
x1 = b - gr*(b-a)
x2 = a + gr*(b-a)
f1=f(x1); f2=f(x2)
for ...:
  if f1 < f2:
    b=x2; x2=x1; f2=f1; x1=b-gr*(b-a); f1=f(x1)
  else:
    a=x1; x1=x2; f1=f2; x2=a+gr*(b-a); f2=f(x2)
t = (a+b)/2
```

Gradient for active peaks:
```
def active_gradient(a,c,K,tau=None):
   idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:] if K< c.size else np.arange(c.size)
   # weights: use softmax(c[idx]/tau), choose tau=max(M/50, 1e-15)
   w = softmax(c[idx]/tau); w /= sum
   grad = zeros(n)
   for k,wt in zip(idx,w):
      if wt <1e-10: continue
      ki=int(k)
      jlo=max(0,ki-n+1); jhi=min(n-1,ki)
      pair = ki - np.arange(jlo,jhi+1)
      grad[jlo:jhi+1] += 2*wt*a[pair]
   return grad, idx, w
```
Loop K up to 64. Source cand = np.argsort(grad)[-S:][::-1], dest cand = np.argsort(grad)[:D]. But dest grad low may include zeros. Ensure source>0.

        tried=0
        improved=False

             if not active_decreasing(s,d): continue

Parametric loss/grad:
```
def parametric_curve(theta, x_shape, feats, n, tau):
    f = feats @ theta
    m = f.max()
    e = np.exp(f - m)
    a = e / e.sum()
    return a

def parametric_loss_grad(theta, feats, n, tau, max_terms):
    f = feats @ theta
    # stable exp
    f -= f.max()
    e = np.exp(f); a = e / e.sum()
    c = fft_convolution(a)
    # loss = tau*logsumexp(c/tau)
    scaled = c / tau - (c.max()/tau)
    zz = np.exp(scaled)
    s = zz.sum()
    loss = tau * (c.max()/tau + np.log(s)) = c.max() + tau*np.log(s)? Actually logsumexp(c/tau)= c.max()/tau + log(sum exp(c/tau - max/tau)). Multiply tau: c.max() + tau log(sum). Good.
    # weights w = zz / s, but stable: w=exp(scaled)/s
    grad_a = zeros(n)
    # To avoid O(L*n), sort weights descending or threshold.
    # If tau small, top few. Use top K by weight.
    K = min(len(c), max_terms)
    idx = np.argpartition(w, -K)[-K:] if K < len(c) else np.arange(len(c))
    # But w maybe not huge; K=128.
    for k in idx:
       wk=w[k]
       if wk < 1e-12: continue
       ki=int(k); jlo...; grad_a[...] += 2*wk*a[pair]
    # grad theta
    mean_feats = a @ feats
    v = grad_a * a
    total = v.sum()
    grad_theta = v @ feats - total * mean_feats
    return loss, grad_theta
```

def norm_nonneg(a):
  a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
  if a.size==0: return a
  a=np.where(a>0,a,0.0)
  # floor to prevent zeros? maybe no.
  s=a.sum()
  if s<=0: return np.ones_like(a)/a.size
  return a/s
```

def active_gradient(a,c,K=32,tau=None):
  n=len(a); M=c.max()
  if tau is None: tau=max(1e-15,M*0.01) # focus
  L=c.size
  if K>=L:
     idx=np.arange(L)
     sw=np.exp(c/tau - c.max()); w=sw/sw.sum()
  else:
     # choose top K by value? But for weights softmax over all? If τ small, top. Use argpartition top by c.
     idx=np.argpartition(c,L-K)[-K:]
     # weights over top only normalized
     tmp=c[idx]/tau; tmp-=tmp.max(); w=np.exp(tmp); w/=w.sum()
  grad=np.zeros(n)
  # sort idx? maybe descending by w? Doesn't matter
  for ii in range(len(idx)):
     k=int(idx[ii]); wt=float(w[ii])
     if wt<1e-12: continue
     jlo=max(0,k-n+1); jhi=min(n-1,k)
     if jlo>jhi: continue
     pair = k - np.arange(jlo,jhi+1)
     grad[jlo:jhi+1] += 2.0*wt*a[pair]
  return grad, idx
```

Parametric function:
```
def param_loss_grad(theta, feats, n, tau, max_terms=256):
   if not all finite theta: return inf, zeros
   f=np.asarray(feats,dtype=float) @ theta
   if not finite: return inf, zeros
   fmax=f.max(); if not finite...; f=f-fmax
   e=np.exp(f)
   # if all zero? no
   s=e.sum(); if s<=0: return inf, zeros
   a=e/s
   c=fft_convolution(a)
   M=c.max(); tau_safe=max(float(tau),1e-15)
   scaled=c/tau_safe - M/tau_safe
   z=np.exp(scaled); ssum=z.sum()
   if ssum<=0 or not finite: return inf, zeros
   loss=M + tau_safe*np.log(ssum)
   w=z/ssum
   grad_a=np.zeros(n)
   # Choose top weights. Since w.sum=1, top K by w.
   K=min(max_terms, c.size)
   if K>=c.size: idx=np.arange(c.size)
   else: idx=np.argpartition(w, c.size-K)[c.size-K:]
   # To loop in descending not necessary. But if w small skip.
   for ii in idx:
      wk=float(w[ii]); if wk<1e-12: continue; k=int(ii) ...
   mean_feats=a @ feats
   v=grad_a*a
   total=v.sum()
   grad_theta=v @ feats - total*mean_feats
   if not finite grad_theta: grad_theta=0? return loss, zeros? Maybe scale.
   return loss, grad_theta
```
But note if constant feature excluded, mean_feats okay. If features include constant? no.

Let's estimate: For a peak, a_pair~1e-4, times2, w one ->2e-4. grad_a_j ~2e-4. v=grad_a_j*a_j ~2e-8 per j, sum n=4000 =>8e-5. grad_theta ~1e-4. Loss 1e-4. Step sizes okay. def coordinate_refine(a0, time_limit, start_time, max_no_improve=12, pair_budget=25, evals=22, verbose=False):
    t0=start_time
    a=norm_nonneg(a0)
    c=fft_convolution(a)
    current_m=c.max()
    best_a=a.copy(); best_m=current_m
    rng=np.random.default_rng(123)
    no_imp=0
    iteration=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit and no_imp < max_no_improve:
       iteration+=1
       # active idx top 16, gradient K=48
       grad, grad_idx = active_gradient(a,c,K=48)
       active_idx = np.argpartition(c, -min(16,c.size))[-min(16,c.size):]
       # sources: top 5 grad; dests: bottom 5 grad
       order = np.argsort(grad)
       dests = order[:5]
       sources = order[-5:][::-1]
       # ensure source value >0
       line_results=[]
       considered=0
       for s in sources:
          si=int(s)
          if a[si] <= 1e-300: continue
          for d in dests:
             di=int(d)
             if si==di: continue
             considered +=1
             if considered > pair_budget: break
             if active_direction_ok(a,c,si,di,active_idx,tol=1e-12):
                line_res = pair_line_search(a,c,si,di,current_m,evals=evals)
                if line_res[1] < current_m - 1e-14:
                    line_results.append(line_res)
          if considered > pair_budget: break
       # also random 5 pairs
       for _ in range(5): ...
       if line_results:
          line_results.sort(key=lambda x: x[0]) # Lambda forbidden! Need no lambda. We can sort using `line_results.sort()` if tuple first is best_f. Good. No lambda.
          accepted=False
          for item in line_results[:4]:
             _,best_t,si,di,L_arr,q1,q2,q3? We can store tuple (best_f,best_t,si,di,L,q1,q2,q3). To avoid storing L for all, maybe tuple with item.
             trial, c2, M2 = apply_pair_move(a,best_t,si,di)
             if M2 < current_m - 1e-15:
                a=trial; c=c2; current_m=M2; best_a=a.copy(); best_m=M2; no_imp=0; accepted=True; break
          if accepted: continue
       # no improvement
       no_imp +=1
       # Try random coordinate moves with smaller line search
       accepted=False
       for _ in range(8):
          si=int(rng.integers(len(a)))
          di=int(rng.integers(len(a)))
          if si==di or a[si]<=0: continue
          # maybe choose source from top half values? Use grad top/bottom random
          line_res=pair_line_search(a,c,si,di,current_m,evals=12)
          if line_res[1] < current_m - 1e-13:
             trial,c2,M2=apply...
             if M2<current_m -1e-15: accept update; break
       if not accepted:
          # perturb after several stalls
          if no_imp % 2 ==0 or no_imp > 3:
             strength=0.02 + 0.01*min(no_imp,5)
             a = perturb_sequence(best_a, rng, strength)
             c=fft; current_m=c.max(); if current_m < best_m: best...; # keep current even worse
             no_imp=0
          else:
             no_imp +=1
    return best_a, best_m
```

def build_basis(n, kind=0):

       deg=12

       v=np.polynomial.chebyshev.chebvander(x, deg)

`parametric_run`:
```
def parametric_run(n, init_a, basis_kind, max_iters1, max_iters2, tau_factor1=0.5, tau_factor2=0.05, bounds_lo=-6, bounds_hi=6):
    a0=norm_nonneg(init_a)
    c0=fft_convolution(a0); M0=c0.max()
    feats=build_basis(n,basis_kind)
    theta=fit_theta(a0,feats); clip bounds
    # Ensure finite
    best_theta=theta[:]; best_a=a0; best_score=local_score(a0)
    for stage,tau_factor,iters in [(1,tau_factor1,max_iters1),(2,tau_factor2,max_iters2)]:
       # update M from best_a
       M=local_M(best_a)
       tau=max(1e-12, M*tau_factor)
       args=(feats,n,tau,128)
       res=sco.minimize(param_loss_grad, best_theta, jac=True, args=args, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=...)
       if res.x finite: a=param_a(res.x); score=local_score; if score<best_score: update
    return best_a,best_score
```

`norm_nonneg`:
```
def norm_nonneg(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if arr.size==0:
       return arr
    arr[~np.isfinite(arr)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(arr, 0.0, out=arr)
    # optional floor? if all zero
    s=arr.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones_like(arr)/arr.size
    arr /= s
    return arr
```
If input contains large >1000? Parent maybe; normalize.

`fft_convolution`:
```
def fft_convolution(a):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.zeros(0)
    size=2*n-1
    N=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, N)[:size]
    # roundoff neg
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0? If many negative due error? set.
    return c
```
If set all negative to 0, it might artificially lower max? Negative only small. Good.

`local_M`:
```
def local_M(a):
   a=norm_nonneg(a)
   c=fft_convolution(a)
   return float(c.max()) if c.size else inf
```
`local_score`:
```
def local_score(a):
   a=norm_nonneg(a)
   c=fft_convolution(a)
   return float(2*a.size*c.max())
```
Since we normalize in local. If sequence already normalized, okay.

`fit_theta`:
```
def fit_theta(a, feats):
    a=norm_nonneg(a)
    loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    if feats.shape[1]==0: return np.zeros(0)
    sol=np.linalg.lstsq(feats, loga, rcond=None)[0]
    sol=np.nan_to_num(sol, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return sol
```
Clip in run.

`param_a`:
```
def param_a(theta, feats):
    f = np.asarray(feats, float) @ np.asarray(theta, float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(f)):
       # fallback uniform
       return np.ones(feats.shape[0]) / feats.shape[0]
    f = f - f.max()
    e = np.exp(f)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.ones(feats.shape[0]) / feats.shape[0]
    return e/s
```

`param_loss_grad`: Need `n` maybe equals feats.shape[0]. Use max_terms.
```
def param_loss_grad(theta, feats, n, tau, max_terms):
   if theta.size==0:
      a=np.ones(n)/n ...
   else:
      f=feats@theta
   ...
   c=fft_convolution(a)
   Mc=float(c.max())
   tau=float(tau)
   if not np.isfinite(tau) or tau <= 0: tau=1e-12
   shifted=c/tau - Mc/tau
   z=np.exp(shifted)
   sumz=z.sum()
   if sumz<=0 or not finite: return inf, zeros
   loss=Mc + tau*np.log(sumz)
   w=z/sumz
   grad_a=np.zeros(n)
   K=int(max_terms)
   L=c.size
   if K >= L: idx=np.arange(L)
   else: idx=np.argpartition(w, L-K)[L-K:]
   # loop; maybe sort by weight desc? Could not. We can skip low.
   for ii in range(idx.size):
      wk=float(w[int(idx[ii])])
      if wk < 1e-12: continue
      k=int(idx[ii])
      jlo=max(0,k-n+1); jhi=min(n-1,k)
      if jlo>jhi: continue
      p = k - np.arange(jlo, jhi+1)
      grad_a[jlo:jhi+1] += 2.0*wk*a[p]
   mean_feats = a @ feats
   v = grad_a * a
   total = float(v.sum())
   grad_theta = v @ feats - total*mean_feats
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_theta)):
       grad_theta = np.zeros_like(theta)
   return float(loss), grad_theta
```
Potential: If `theta` size differs from feats columns? Should not. `v @ feats` length d. If d=0, return zeros.

Note: `mean_feats = a @ feats` shape d. Good.

`active_gradient`: Need choose `tau`? We can simply use weights proportional to sorted c maybe. For descent, weights can be `(c/M)**p`? To focus active near max but smooth. ```
def active_gradient(a,c,K=48,tau_factor=0.02):
   L=c.size; n=a.size; M=c.max()
   K=min(max(1,K),L)

   # But if M=0 uniform? tau 1e-12.

   w=np.exp(scaled); sumw=w.sum(); if sumw<=0: w=np.ones_like(w)/w.size else w/=sumw
   grad=zeros
   for ii in idx: ...
   return grad, idx
```

`active_direction_ok` vectorized:
```
def active_direction_ok(a,c,s,d,active_idx,tol=1e-12):
    n=a.size
    if active_idx.size==0: return True
    ks=active_idx.astype(np.int64)
    ps=ks - s
    qs=ks - d
    lvals=np.zeros(ks.size)
    mp=(ps>=0)&(ps<n)
    if np.any(mp): lvals[mp] -= 2.0*a[ps[mp]]
    mq=(qs>=0)&(qs<n)
    if np.any(mq): lvals[mq] += 2.0*a[qs[mq]]
    return float(np.max(lvals)) <= float(tol)
```
But if s,d large, ps arrays. Good.

`eval_pair_t`:
```
def eval_pair_t(c,L,t,q1,q2,q3):
    tmp = c + t*L
    if q1>=0: tmp[q1] += t*t
    if q2>=0: tmp[q2] += t*t
    if q3>=0: tmp[q3] += 2.0*t*t
    return float(tmp.max())
```
Could be slow due allocating. Fine.

`pair_line_search`:
```
def pair_line_search(a,c,s,d,M,evals=22):
    n=a.size; Llen=c.size
    L=np.zeros(Llen, dtype=np.float64)
    if a[s] <= 0: return None
    tmax=float(a[s])
    if tmax <= 0: return None
    L[s:s+n] -= 2.0*a
    L[d:d+n] += 2.0*a
    q1=2*s if 2*s < Llen else -1
    q2=2*d if 2*d < Llen else -1
    q3=s+d if s+d < Llen else -1
    # evaluate endpoints
    f0=float(M) # maybe eval? Use M
    fend=eval_pair_t(c,L,tmax,q1,q2,q3)
    best_f=min(f0,fend); best_t=0.0 if f0<=fend else tmax
    lo=0.0; hi=tmax; gr=0.686134; gold=0.618034
    x1 = hi - gold*(hi-lo)
    x2 = lo + gold*(hi-lo)
    f1=eval_pair_t(c,L,x1,q1,q2,q3)
    f2=eval_pair_t(c,L,x2,q1,q2,q3)
    if f1<best_f: best_f=f1; best_t=x1
    if f2<best_f: best_f=f2; best_t=x2
    for _ in range(evals):
       if f1 < f2:
          hi=x2; x2=x1; f2=f1; x1=hi - gold*(hi-lo); f1=eval...
       else:
          lo=x1; x1=x2; f1=f2; x2=lo + gold*(hi-lo); f2=eval...
       if f1<best_f: ...
       if f2<best_f: ...
    return (best_f, best_t, s,d,L,q1,q2,q3)
```
But note if best endpoint tmax and f0 maybe not true due M; if c max from FFT, f(0)=M exactly. Good. If tmax=0, return None. If best_t=0 no improvement. We only accept if best_f < M - tol.

Potential: Golden search on convex function with min at boundary returns boundary but due f0/fend eval, okay.

But `L` array passed to line item; when apply, use s,d,t. It doesn't need L. We can avoid storing L by not storing? For exact apply only need t,s,d. We can store no L. The tuple after line search could be `(best_f, idx, best_t, s, d)`. No arrays; sort safe. Then exact apply uses a,s,d. We don't need L after scoring. Good! We can discard L. Pair_line_search can return only `(best_f,best_t,s,d)` to save memory. But to sort no arrays. Then exact. Great.
`pair_line_search` returns tuple or None.

But exact apply recomputes FFT. If line best_f was best but exact c not improve due inconsistency, try next.

`apply_pair_move`:
```
def apply_pair_move(a,s,d,t):
   a2=a.copy()
   a2[s]-=t
   a2[d]+=t
   a2=np.maximum(a2,0.0)
   ssum=a2.sum()
   if ssum <= 1e-300: return None
   a2 /= ssum
   c2=fft_convolution(a2)
   return a2,c2,float(c2.max())
```
Check if t too big: t<=a[s] exact maybe but due float a2[s] slightly negative, max clips losing mass -> sum < original; normalize scales. But if t close a[s], clipping okay. Could cause score change not line; exact M catches.

`perturb_sequence`:
```
def perturb_sequence(a, rng, strength):
   noise = rng.normal(0.0, strength, a.size)
   b = a + noise*a
   b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
   b /= b.sum() if >0 else uniform
   return b
```
Maybe multiplicative log perturbation: `np.exp(rng.normal(0,strength))`. Use multiplicative to keep nonneg:
```
r = rng.uniform(1-strength,1+strength,size)
b=a*r; normalize
```
Strength small. If strength<1. Good.

`coordinate_refine`: Need ensure not infinite due no_improve reset on perturbation. We'll implement robust. Pseudocode detailed:
```
def coordinate_refine(a0, time_limit, start_time, max_no_improve=15, pair_budget=24, evals=20, verbose=False):
    t_start=start_time
    a=norm_nonneg(a0)
    c=fft_convolution(a); current_m=float(c.max())
    best_a=a.copy(); best_m=current_m
    rng=np.random.default_rng(42)
    no_improve=0
    iteration=0
    while time.monotonic()-t_start < time_limit and no_improve < max_no_improve:
       iteration += 1
       # recompute gradient
       grad, grad_idx = active_gradient(a,c,K=48)
       top_active_count = min(16, c.size)
       active_idx = np.argpartition(c, c.size-top_active_count)[c.size-top_active_count:]
       # sources: top 6 grad, but filter a>0
       src_all = np.argsort(grad)
       sources = src_all[-6:][::-1]
       dests = src_all[:6]
       items=[]
       considered=0
       # Add top grad to low grad combos
       for s in sources:
          si=int(s)
          if a[si] <= 1e-300 or considered >= pair_budget: break
          for d in dests:
             di=int(d)
             if si==di or considered >= pair_budget: continue
             considered +=1
             if active_direction_ok(a,c,si,di,active_idx,tol=1e-12):
                r=pair_line_search(a,c,si,di,current_m,evals=evals)
                if r is not None and r[0] < current_m - 1e-14:
                    items.append((float(r[0]), len(items), float(r[1]), si, di))
       # If none, try random ordered pairs without active precheck, maybe to escape
       if not items:
          for _ in range(min(12, pair_budget)):
             si=int(rng.integers(a.size))
             di=int(rng.integers(a.size))
             if si==di or a[si]<=1e-300: continue
             # To bias source high, dest low: if grad[si] < grad[di], swap? Maybe.
             if grad[si] < grad[di]:
                tmp=si; si=di; di=tmp
             r=pair_line_search(a,c,si,di,current_m,evals=12)
             if r is not None and r[0] < current_m - 1e-14:
                items.append((float(r[0]), len(items), float(r[1]), si, di))
       accepted=False
       if items:
          items.sort()
          for item in items[:5]:
             _, _, t, si, di = item
             if t <= 1e-15: continue
             res=apply_pair_move(a,si,di,t)
             if res is not None:
                trial,c2,M2=res
                if M2 < current_m - 1e-15:
                   a=trial; c=c2; current_m=M2
                   if M2 < best_m - 1e-15:
                      best_a=a.copy(); best_m=M2
                   no_improve=0; accepted=True
                   if verbose: print...
                   break
       if accepted:
          continue
       no_improve +=1
       # random moves direct smaller evals
       accepted=False
       for _ in range(10):
          choose source from top grad half, dest bottom half:
             si_order = np.argsort(grad) # but each loop expensive; we can precompute
             si = int(si_order[-(rng.integers(1, len(a)//2+1))])? Hard.
          simpler random with gradient bias:
             si=int(rng.integers(a.size)); di=int(rng.integers(a.size)); if grad[si] < grad[di]: swap
          r=pair_line_search(... evals=10)
          if improves exact: accept
       if not accepted:
          if no_improve >= 3:
             strength = 0.03 + 0.01*min(no_improve,5)
             a=perturb_sequence(best_a, rng, strength)
             c=fft_convolution(a); current_m=float(c.max())
             if current_m < best_m - 1e-15:
                best_a=a.copy(); best_m=current_m
                no_improve=0
             else:
                no_improve = max(1, no_improve-1) # allow few more? But to eventually stop, reset to 0.
                no_improve=0
          else:
             no_improve +=1 # more stalls
       if verbose and iteration%10==0: print...
    return best_a,best_m
```
Need avoid too aggressive no_improve increment twice. Simpler:
```
if not accepted:
  no_improve +=1
  if no_improve > 5:
     perturb; no_improve=0; continue
  # try random next loop
```
This may loop. We'll set `stall` increments; when stall reaches 3, random; 6 perturb; 10 perturb bigger; max_no_improve 20? If no improvement after many random, perturb.

Function:
```
def full_loss_grad(theta, n, tau, max_terms):
   # a = softmax(theta)
   f=theta (if theta too large, subtract max)
   f-=np.max(f)
   e=np.exp(f); a=e/e.sum()
   c=fft(a); loss=tau*logsumexp(c/tau)
   weights w top max_terms
   grad_a as before
   # Since features identity, mean_feats = a
   v=grad_a*a; total=v.sum(); grad_theta = v - total*a
   return loss, grad_theta
```

def full_loss_grad(theta, n, tau, max_terms):
   f=np.asarray(theta,float)
   if not finite: return inf, zeros
   f=f-f.max()
   e=np.exp(f); s=e.sum(); if s<=0 return inf...
   a=e/s
   c=fft; M=c.max(); ... loss,w
   grad_a top K
   v=grad_a*a; total=v.sum(); grad=v-total*a
   return loss, grad
```

Given a normalized f, M=max f*f. We want solve LP maximize ∑g_j subject f*g ≤ M, g≥0. Because constraints linear in g. There is a dual (covering). Direction g can be found by solving dual approximately with multiplicative weights. We can implement a few hundred iterations of multiplicative weights to solve dual, then complementary slackness? But maybe we can use the dual solution y to define update for f: KKT for LP? Let's see.

Primal LP: max 1^T g
s.t. (f*g)_k ≤ M, g≥0.
Dual: min M 1^T y
s.t. (f*rev? A^T y)_j ≥ 1, y≥0.
At optimum, complementary: y_k (f*g)_k=0, g_j (A^T y_j -1)=0. If we could find y, support of g where A^T y =1. But solving y covering LP. def lp_direction(a, add_reg=1e-4, max_n=1500):
    n=len(a)
    if n > max_n: a=interpolate_to_n(a,max_n); n=max_n
    a_reg = (1-add_reg)*a + add_reg/n # full support normalized? sum =1
    M = local_M(a_reg) # maybe use local_M recomputes norm; okay
    A=build_conv_A(a_reg)
    c_obj=-np.ones(n)

    g=np.maximum(g,0); s=g.sum(); if s<=0: g=a_reg
    g/=s
    return g

Detailed function writing:

```
def norm_nonneg(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = np.ravel(arr)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return arr
    arr[~np.isfinite(arr)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(arr, 0.0, out=arr)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(arr) / float(arr.size)
    arr /= s
    return arr
```
If arr from list of booleans? Evaluation rejects booleans but we don't output. Good.

`fft_convolution`:
```
def fft_convolution(a):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2*n - 1
    N = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:size]
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c)
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c
```
If c has negative due true? true nonnegative. Good.

`local_M` and `local_score`.

`interpolate_to_n`: If a is subarray and new_n equal return copy.
```
def interpolate_to_n(a,new_n):
    a=norm_nonneg(a)
    old_n=a.size
    if new_n <=0: return a
    if old_n == new_n: return a.copy()
    old_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n)
    new_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
    b=np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    return norm_nonneg(b)
```
For old_n=1, linspace [0]. interp okay. `norm_nonneg` if sum >0.

def make_power_ramp(n, power):
   x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   a=(x+1e-9)**power + 1e-12
   return norm_nonneg(a)
```

    try:
       if kind==0:
          deg=14; x=...; v=np.polynomial.chebyshev.chebvander(x, deg); return v[:,1:]
       if kind==1:

       pass

```

`lp_search`:
```
def lp_search(a0, time_limit, start_time, n_lp=1024, n_iters=6, attach=False):
    a=interpolate_to_n(a0,n_lp); best=a.copy(); best_m=local_M(a)
    if not HAVE_SCIPY: return best
    Mcur=best_m
    for it in range(n_iters):
       if time.monotonic()-start_time > time_limit: break
       reg = 1e-4
       a_reg = (1-reg)*best + (reg/float(n_lp))
       a_reg = norm_nonneg(a_reg)
       Mreg = local_M(a_reg)
       A = build_conv_A(a_reg)
       cobj = -np.ones(n_lp)
       b = Mreg * np.ones(2*n_lp-1)
       bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n_lp
       g = None
       methods = ['highs', 'highs-ds', 'interior-point', 'revised simplex', 'simplex']
       for m in methods:
          try:
             res = sco.linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method=m, options={'presolve': True})
             if res.success and res.x is not None:
                g=np.asarray(res.x,float)
                break
          except Exception:
             pass
       if g is None:
          # fallback: g = a_reg
          g=a_reg.copy()
       g=np.maximum(g,0); s=g.sum(); if s<=0: g=a_reg
       g /= s
       # line search
       fractions=[0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.75,1.0]
       best_f=Mcur; best_candidate=None; best_lambda=0.0
       for lam in fractions:
           cand = (1.0-lam)*best + lam*g
           cand = norm_nonneg(cand)
           mval=local_M(cand)
           if mval < best_f:
              best_f=mval; best_candidate=cand; best_lambda=lam
       if best_candidate is not None:
           best=best_candidate; Mcur=best_f
       else:
           # tiny random perturbation maybe? if no direction improve
           # break if it>1?
           break
    return best
```
But if reg makes Mreg > Mbest, direction feasible with larger constraints; good. If g from LP has sum maybe very large? Normalized. Line search local_M each uses FFT. 9*iters. Fine.

Now, coordinate refine: Need pass `start_time` absolute and `time_limit` relative. We should compute time left before calling. In coordinate, if `time.monotonic()-start_time < time_limit`. It also uses `time.monotonic()` inside loops. Good.

Detailed coordinate code must be syntactically correct. Let's draft it fully:
```
def coordinate_refine(a0, time_limit, start_time, max_no_improve=15, pair_budget=24, evals=20, verbose=False):
    t_start = start_time
    a = norm_nonneg(a0)
    n = a.size
    c = fft_convolution(a)
    current_m = float(c.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = current_m
    rng = np.random.default_rng(2024)
    stall = 0
    iteration = 0
    half = max(1, n // 2)
    while time.monotonic() - t_start < time_limit and stall < max_no_improve:
        iteration += 1
        # gradient
        grad, grad_idx = active_gradient(a, c, K=48)
        active_count = min(16, c.size)
        if active_count <= 0:
            active_idx = np.array([], dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            active_idx = np.argpartition(c, c.size - active_count)[c.size - active_count:]
        order = np.argsort(grad)
        source_list = order[-min(6, n):][::-1]
        dest_list = order[:min(6, n)]
        items = []
        considered = 0
        # systematic
        for s in source_list:
            si = int(s)
            if a[si] <= 1e-300 or considered >= pair_budget:
                break
            for d in dest_list:
                di = int(d)
                if si == di or considered >= pair_budget:
                    continue
                considered += 1
                if active_direction_ok(a, c, si, di, active_idx, 1e-12):
                    r = pair_line_search(a, c, si, di, current_m, evals=evals)
                    if r is not None and r[0] < current_m - 1e-14:
                        items.append((float(r[0]), len(items), float(r[1]), si, di))
        # random if none
        if not items:
            low_half = order[:half]
            high_half = order[half:]
            for _ in range(min(12, pair_budget)):
                si = int(high_half[int(rng.integers(high_half.size))])
                di = int(low_half[int(rng.integers(low_half.size))])
                if si == di or a[si] <= 1e-300:
                    continue
                r = pair_line_search(a, c, si, di, current_m, evals=12)
                if r is not None and r[0] < current_m - 1e-13:
                    items.append((float(r[0]), len(items), float(r[1]), si, di))
        accepted = False
        if items:
            items.sort()
            for item in items[:5]:
                if time.monotonic() - t_start > time_limit:
                    break
                _, _, tval, si, di = item
                if tval <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                res = apply_pair_move(a, si, di, tval)
                if res is not None:
                    trial, c2, m2 = res
                    if m2 < current_m - 1e-15:
                        a = trial; c = c2; current_m = float(m2)
                        if current_m < best_m - 1e-15:
                            best_a = a.copy(); best_m = current_m
                        accepted = True
                        stall = 0
                        if verbose and iteration % 20 == 0:
                            print(...)
                        break
        if accepted:
            continue
        stall += 1
        # random direct small
        for _ in range(8):
            if time.monotonic() - t_start > time_limit:
                break
            si = int(order[half + int(rng.integers(half))]) # if half length? order[half:] size n-half
            di = int(order[int(rng.integers(half))])
            if si == di or a[si] <= 1e-300:
                continue
            # ensure source better than dest? if grad[si] < grad[di], swap
            if grad[si] < grad[di]:
                tmp = si; si = di; di = tmp
            r = pair_line_search(a, c, si, di, current_m, evals=10)
            if r is not None and r[0] < current_m - 1e-13:
                res = apply_pair_move(a, si, di, float(r[1]))
                if res is not None:
                    trial, c2, m2 = res
                    if m2 < current_m - 1e-15:
                        a = trial; c = c2; current_m = float(m2)
                        if current_m < best_m - 1e-15:
                            best_a = a.copy(); best_m = current_m
                        break
        else:
            if stall > 5:
                strength = 0.04 + 0.015 * min(stall, 8)
                a = perturb_sequence(best_a, rng, strength)
                c = fft_convolution(a)
                current_m = float(c.max())
                if current_m < best_m - 1e-15:
                    best_a = a.copy(); best_m = current_m
                stall = 0
                continue
            # if not perturb, just loop? Need avoid infinite with no a change. We'll continue, but next iteration same. The for-else will perturb only after stall>5. If stall <=5, it will loop with same a and stall increment each time. Good.
    return best_a, float(best_m)
```
But Python `for ... else`: if break due time, else executes? No, if break due time, else skipped. If loop completes no improvement, else. Good.
However in random direct, if break after improvement, we do `continue` outer? In code, after random direct if improvement, we should set accepted? The outer after for-else will continue if not else. If break, for-else not executed, then falls to next iteration. Good. Need ensure stall reset if improvement. But in random direct improvement block, we didn't set stall=0. Add.
Also if improvement in items block, accepted true and continue. Good.

In random selection: `order[half + int(rng.integers(half))]` maybe source from second half of size n-half. If n=4096, half=2048, high indices 2048..4095 size 2048; rng.integers(half) returns 0..2047, index 2048..4095 ok. For dest `order[int(rng.integers(half))]`. Good. But if n odd? half=floor; high size n-half. rng range half might be less than high size by up to 1; okay biased but fine. `perturb_sequence` top:
```
def perturb_sequence(a, rng, strength):
   if strength <= 0: return a.copy()
   r = rng.uniform(1.0 - strength, 1.0 + strength, a.size)
   b = a * r
   b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
   s = b.sum()
   if s <= 0: return a.copy()
   b /= s
   return b
```
If strength>1, lower bound negative; keep strength <=0.5. We use max 0.16. Good.

Now, `full_lbfgs_refine` details:
```
def full_loss_grad(theta, n, tau, max_terms):
    if n <=0: return inf, zeros
    th = np.asarray(theta,float)
    if th.size != n or not finite: return inf, zeros
    thc = np.clip(th, -50, 50)
    f = thc - thc.max()
    e = np.exp(f); s=e.sum(); if s<=0: return inf, zeros
    a=e/s
    c=fft_convolution(a)
    M=float(c.max()); tau=max(float(tau),1e-15)
    scaled=c/tau - M/tau; z=np.exp(scaled); sumz=z.sum(); if bad return inf
    loss=M+tau*np.log(sumz)
    w=z/sumz
    grad_a=zeros
    K=min(int(max_terms), c.size)
    if K>=c.size: idx=np.arange(c.size)
    else: idx=np.argpartition(w, c.size-K)[c.size-K:]
    for ii in idx: k=int(ii); wk=float(w[k]); if wk<1e-12 continue; jlo...
    v=grad_a*a; total=v.sum(); grad=v - total*a
    mask=(theta>-50)&(theta<50)
    grad=grad*mask
    grad=np.nan_to_num(grad)
    return float(loss), grad
```
If all theta at bounds, mask maybe false for some; gradient zero. Good.

`full_lbfgs_refine`:
```
def full_lbfgs_refine(a0, iters1, iters2, tau1_factor=0.1, tau2_factor=0.02):
   a=norm_nonng(a0); if a.size > 8000? handle by caller
   # floor
   a_floor = np.maximum(a, 1e-12 / a.size)
   a = norm_nonneg(a_floor)
   theta = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
   best_a=a; best_sc=local_score(a)
   if not HAVE_SCIPY: return best_a,best_sc
   theta_best=theta.copy()
   stages=[(tau1_factor,it1),(tau2_factor,it2)]
   for factor,iters in stages:
      M=local_M(best_a)
      tau=max(1e-12, M*factor)
      args=(a.size, tau, 128)
      try:
        res=sco.minimize(full_loss_grad, theta_best, args=args, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter':iters,'maxfun':iters,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-11})
        if res.x finite:
           # softmax with clip
           thc=np.clip(res.x, -50,50); f=thc-thc.max(); e=np.exp(f); cand=e/e.sum()
           sc=local_score(cand)
           if sc<best_sc:
              best_a=cand; best_sc=sc; theta_best=res.x.copy()
      except: pass
   return best_a,best_sc
```
Will theta_best from best sequence not necessarily exactly log(best_a) if best updated from res? okay. But if theta_best goes outside, softmax uses clip; fine. If stage2 M uses best_a. Good.

Need be careful: `full_loss_grad` first arg theta, then args (n,tau,max_terms). SciPy calls `func(theta, n, tau, max_terms)`. Good.

Potential issue: L-BFGS-B with `jac=True` and `args` works if function returns tuple. Yes.

Let's write `try_update`:
```
def try_update(best_seq, best_score, a, label, t_start):
    if a is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    arr = norm_nonneg(a)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return best_seq, best_score
    sc = local_score(arr)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
        print("  NEW BEST %s: %.8f n=%d t=%.0f" % (label, sc, arr.size, elapsed))
        return arr.copy(), float(sc)
    return best_seq, best_score
```
No closure. If best_seq None initial, best_score inf. Good.

Potential: Calling `local_score` normalizes again but `arr` normalized. Good.

`interpolate_to_n`: If new_n is float? use int. `new_n = int(new_n)`. if <=0 return empty? But we don't call. Use if <=0 return a.

```
def interpolate_to_n(a, new_n):
    new_n = int(new_n)
    a = norm_nonneg(a)
    if new_n <= 0: return a
    old_n = a.size
    if new_n == old_n: return a.copy()
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
    b = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    return norm_nonneg(b)
```
If old_n=0, linspace(0,1,0) okay, interp with empty? Could fail. But no empty. To be safe: if old_n==0 return np.ones(new_n)/new_n. Add.

```python
"""
Searches for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2
with a multi-scale, multi-method optimizer:
- fast exact coordinate line-search moves
- low-dimensional parametric smoothing (Chebyshev/Gaussian bases)
- full-dimensional L-BFGS on log-softmax parameters
- sparse/dense LP Frank-Wolfe-style updates from the current point
- differential evolution for shape discovery at small lengths
- length interpolation and final local polishing
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    import scipy.optimize as sco
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False


def norm_nonneg(a):
    """Normalize a sequence to non-negative entries with sum one."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = np.ravel(arr)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return arr
    arr[~np.isfinite(arr)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(arr, 0.0, out=arr)
    np.minimum(arr, 1000.0, out=arr)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(arr) / float(arr.size)
    arr /= s
    return arr


def fft_convolution(a):
    """Exact linear convolution a*a via zero-padded FFT."""
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:size]
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c)
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c


def local_M(a):
    """Return max convolution coefficient for normalized a."""
    a = norm_nonneg(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = fft_convolution(a)
    return float(c.max())


def local_score(a):
    """Return the objective for normalized a: 2*n*max(conv(a,a))."""
    a = norm_nonneg(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = fft_convolution(a)
    return float(2.0 * a.size * float(c.max()))


def interpolate_to_n(a, new_n):
    """Interpolate a normalized sequence to a new length."""
    new_n = int(new_n)
    a = norm_nonneg(a)
    if new_n <= 0:
        return a
    old_n = int(a.size)
    if old_n == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    if new_n == old_n:
        return a.copy()
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    b = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    return norm_nonneg(b)


def make_power_ramp(n, power):
    """A smooth increasing template."""
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    a = (x + 1e-9) ** float(power) + 1e-12
    return norm_nonneg(a)


def try_update(best_seq, best_score, a, label, t_start):
    """Normalize/candidate and update best if it improves."""
    if a is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    arr = norm_nonneg(a)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return best_seq, best_score
    sc = local_score(arr)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < float(best_score):
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
        print("  NEW BEST %s: %.8f  n=%d  t=%.0fs" % (label, sc, arr.size, elapsed))
        return arr.copy(), float(sc)
    return best_seq, best_score


def make_basis(n, kind):
    """Feature matrix for parametric log-density models."""
    n = int(n)
    kind = int(kind)
    if kind == 0:
        try:
            deg = 14
            x = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n)
            v = np.polynomial.chebyshev.chebvander(x, deg)
            return np.ascontiguousarray(v[:, 1:])
        except Exception:
            pass
    if kind == 1:
        try:
            deg = 10
            x = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n)
            v = np.polynomial.chebyshev.chebvander(x, deg)
            xc = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            ng = 14
            g = np.zeros((n, ng), dtype=np.float64)
            denom = float(ng - 1) if ng > 1 else 1.0
            for i in range(ng):
                center = 0.05 + 0.90 * (float(i) / denom)
                width = 0.13 - 0.06 * (float(i) / denom) + (0.03 if (i % 2) else 0.0)
                if width < 0.02:
                    width = 0.02
                g[:, i] = np.exp(-0.5 * ((xc - center) / width) ** 2)
            return np.ascontiguousarray(np.hstack((v[:, 1:], g)))
        except Exception:
            pass
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    feats = np.empty((n, 8), dtype=np.float64)
    for r in range(8):
        feats[:, r] = x ** (r + 1)
    return feats


def fit_theta(a, feats):
    """Least-squares fit of log(a) to features."""
    d = int(feats.shape[1])
    if d == 0 or a.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    a = norm_nonneg(a)
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    try:
        sol = np.linalg.lstsq(feats, loga, rcond=None)[0]
        sol = np.asarray(sol, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        sol = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
    sol = np.where(np.isfinite(sol), sol, 0.0)
    return sol


def param_a(theta, feats):
    """Probability vector from linear log parameters."""
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(feats.shape[0])
    if n == 0:
        return theta
    if theta.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    f = feats @ theta
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(f)):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    f -= float(f.max())
    e = np.exp(f)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return e / s


def param_loss_grad(theta, feats, n, tau, max_terms):
    """Smooth max objective and gradient for parametric basis."""
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    d = int(feats.shape[1])
    n = int(n)
    if theta.size != d:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
    a = param_a(theta, feats)
    if a.size != n:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
    c = fft_convolution(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
    Mc = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(Mc) or Mc <= 0.0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
    tau = float(tau)
    if not np.isfinite(tau) or tau <= 0.0:
        tau = 1e-12
    shifted = c / tau - Mc / tau
    shifted = np.minimum(shifted, 0.0)
    z = np.exp(shifted)
    sz = float(z.sum())
    if sz <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sz):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
    loss = Mc + tau * float(np.log(sz))
    w = z / sz

    grad_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    K = int(max_terms)
    if K <= 0:
        K = 1
    Lc = int(c.size)
    if K >= Lc:
        idx = np.arange(Lc, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(w, Lc - K)[Lc - K:]
    for qq in range(idx.size):
        ii = int(idx[qq])
        wk = float(w[ii])
        if wk < 1e-12:
            continue
        jlo = max(0, ii - n + 1)
        jhi = min(n - 1, ii)
        if jlo > jhi:
            continue
        offs = ii - np.arange(jlo, jhi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        grad_a[jlo:jhi + 1] += 2.0 * wk * a[offs]

    if d == 0:
        return float(loss), np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    mean_feats = a @ feats
    v = grad_a * a
    total = float(v.sum())
    grad_theta = v @ feats - total * mean_feats
    grad_theta = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_theta), grad_theta, 0.0)
    return float(loss), grad_theta.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def param_exact_loss(theta, feats, n):
    """Exact objective for differential evolution."""
    a = param_a(theta, feats)
    c = fft_convolution(a)
    return float(2.0 * n * float(c.max()))


def parametric_run(n, initial_a, basis_kind, max_iter1, max_iter2):
    """Run two-stage L-BFGS on a parametric basis at length n."""
    n = int(n)
    a0 = interpolate_to_n(initial_a, n)
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = local_score(a0)
    if not HAVE_SCIPY or n <= 0:
        return best_a

    feats = make_basis(n, basis_kind)
    d = int(feats.shape[1])
    if d == 0:
        return best_a

    theta = fit_theta(best_a, feats)
    theta = np.clip(theta, -7.0, 7.0)
    theta = np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)
    theta_best = theta.copy()
    bounds = [(-7.0, 7.0)] * d

    stages = ((0.70, int(max_iter1)), (0.08, int(max_iter2)))
    for stage_idx, (factor, iters) in enumerate(stages):
        M = local_M(best_a)
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break
        tau = max(1e-12, M * float(factor))
        args = (feats, n, tau, 220)
        try:
            res = sco.minimize(
                param_loss_grad,
                theta_best,
                args=args,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                options={"maxiter": int(iters), "maxfun": int(max(2, 2 * iters)), "ftol": 1e-15, "gtol": 1e-10},
            )
            if res is not None and res.x is not None:
                th = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                th = np.where(np.isfinite(th), th, 0.0)
                th = np.clip(th, -7.0, 7.0)
                cand = param_a(th, feats)
                sc = local_score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    best_a = cand.copy()
                    theta_best = th.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best_a


def de_param_search(n, initial_a, basis_kind, maxiter=30, popsize=15, seed=123):
    """Differential evolution on a low-dimensional basis at small length."""
    n = int(n)
    a0 = interpolate_to_n(initial_a, n)
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = local_score(a0)
    if not HAVE_SCIPY or n <= 0:
        return best_a

    feats = make_basis(n, basis_kind)
    d = int(feats.shape[1])
    if d == 0:
        return best_a

    bounds = [(-6.0, 6.0)] * d
    try:
        res = sco.differential_evolution(
            param_exact_loss,
            bounds,
            args=(feats, n),
            maxiter=int(maxiter),
            popsize=int(popsize),
            seed=int(seed),
            polish=False,
        )
        if res is not None and res.x is not None:
            th = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            th = np.where(np.isfinite(th), th, 0.0)
            cand = param_a(th, feats)
            sc = local_score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_a = cand.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass
    return best_a


def build_conv_A(a):
    """Dense matrix A with (A*g)_k = (a*g)_k."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j + n, j] = a
    return A


def lp_search(a0, time_limit, start_time, n_lp=1024, n_iters=6):
    """LP Frank-Wolfe-style updates:
       given a, solve max sum(g) s.t. a*g <= max(a*a), g >= 0,
       then line-search along the normalized LP direction.
    """
    a = interpolate_to_n(a0, int(n_lp))
    best = a.copy()
    best_m = local_M(a)
    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return best

    for it in range(int(n_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() - start_time > time_limit:
            break
        n = int(best.size)
        if n <= 0:
            break
        reg = 1e-4
        a_reg = (1.0 - reg) * best + (reg / float(n))
        a_reg = norm_nonneg(a_reg)
        Mreg = local_M(a_reg)
        if not np.isfinite(Mreg) or Mreg <= 0.0:
            break

        try:
            A = build_conv_A(a_reg)
            c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            b_ub = Mreg * np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
            bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
            g = None
            methods = ("highs", "highs-ds", "interior-point", "revised simplex", "simplex")
            for m in methods:
                try:
                    res = sco.linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method=m)
                    if res is not None and getattr(res, "success", False) and res.x is not None:
                        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                        break
                except Exception:
                    pass
            if g is None:
                break
        except Exception:
            break

        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        np.maximum(g, 0.0, out=g)
        gs = float(g.sum())
        if gs <= 1e-300:
            break
        g /= gs

        best_f = best_m
        best_candidate = None
        fractions = (0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00)
        for lam in fractions:
            if time.monotonic() - start_time > time_limit:
                break
            cand = (1.0 - lam) * best + lam * g
            cand = norm_nonneg(cand)
            mval = local_M(cand)
            if np.isfinite(mval) and mval < best_f:
                best_f = float(mval)
                best_candidate = cand

        if best_candidate is not None:
            best = best_candidate.copy()
            best_m = float(best_f)
        else:
            break
    return best


def active_gradient(a, c, K=48, tau_factor=0.02):
    """Weighted subgradient over the most active convolution peaks."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = int(c.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int64)
    M = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        K2 = min(max(1, int(K)), L)
        idx = np.zeros(K2, dtype=np.int64)
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64), idx

    K2 = min(max(1, int(K)), L)
    if K2 >= L:
        idx = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, L - K2)[L - K2:]
    tau = max(1e-30, M * float(tau_factor))
    scaled = (c[idx] - M) / tau
    scaled = np.minimum(scaled, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(scaled)
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        w = np.ones_like(w, dtype=np.float64) / float(w.size)
    else:
        w = w / ws

    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for qq in range(idx.size):
        kk = int(idx[qq])
        wk = float(w[qq])
        if wk < 1e-12:
            continue
        jlo = max(0, kk - n + 1)
        jhi = min(n - 1, kk)
        if jlo > jhi:
            continue
        offs = kk - np.arange(jlo, jhi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        grad[jlo:jhi + 1] += 2.0 * wk * a[offs]
    return grad, idx


def active_direction_ok(a, c, s, d, active_idx, tol=1e-12):
    """Check whether a mass move s->d initially decreases all active peaks."""
    if active_idx is None or active_idx.size == 0:
        return True
    n = int(a.size)
    ks = np.asarray(active_idx, dtype=np.int64)
    ps = ks - int(s)
    qs = ks - int(d)
    lvals = np.zeros(ks.size, dtype=np.float64)
    mp = (ps >= 0) & (ps < n)
    if np.any(mp):
        lvals[mp] -= 2.0 * a[ps[mp]]
    mq = (qs >= 0) & (qs < n)
    if np.any(mq):
        lvals[mq] += 2.0 * a[qs[mq]]
    if lvals.size == 0:
        return True
    return float(np.max(lvals)) <= float(tol)


def eval_pair_t(c, L, t, q1, q2, q3):
    """Evaluation of max convolution coefficient along a coordinate move."""
    tmp = c + float(t) * L
    t2 = float(t) * float(t)
    if int(q1) >= 0:
        tmp[int(q1)] += t2
    if int(q2) >= 0:
        tmp[int(q2)] += t2
    if int(q3) >= 0:
        tmp[int(q3)] += 2.0 * t2
    return float(tmp.max())


def pair_line_search(a, c, s, d, M, evals=22):
    """Golden-section minimization of max c_k(t) for moving mass s->d."""
    if M is None or not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None
    n = int(a.size)
    Llen = int(c.size)
    s = int(s)
    d = int(d)
    if s == d or n == 0 or Llen == 0:
        return None
    tmax = float(a[s])
    if not np.isfinite(tmax) or tmax <= 0.0:
        return None

    L = np.zeros(Llen, dtype=np.float64)
    L[s:s + n] -= 2.0 * a
    L[d:d + n] += 2.0 * a

    q1 = 2 * s if 2 * s < Llen else -1
    q2 = 2 * d if 2 * d < Llen else -1
    q3 = s + d if s + d < Llen else -1

    evals = max(8, int(evals))
    f0 = float(M)
    fend = eval_pair_t(c, L, tmax, q1, q2, q3)
    best_f = min(f0, fend)
    best_t = 0.0 if f0 <= fend else tmax

    lo = 0.0
    hi = float(tmax)
    gold = 0.6180339887498949
    x1 = hi - gold * (hi - lo)
    x2 = lo + gold * (hi - lo)
    f1 = eval_pair_t(c, L, x1, q1, q2, q3)
    f2 = eval_pair_t(c, L, x2, q1, q2, q3)
    if f1 < best_f:
        best_f = f1
        best_t = x1
    if f2 < best_f:
        best_f = f2
        best_t = x2

    for _ in range(evals):
        if f1 < f2:
            hi = x2
            x2 = x1
            f2 = f1
            x1 = hi - gold * (hi - lo)
            f1 = eval_pair_t(c, L, x1, q1, q2, q3)
        else:
            lo = x1
            x1 = x2
            f1 = f2
            x2 = lo + gold * (hi - lo)
            f2 = eval_pair_t(c, L, x2, q1, q2, q3)
        if f1 < best_f:
            best_f = f1
            best_t = x1
        if f2 < best_f:
            best_f = f2
            best_t = x2
        if hi - lo <= 1e-18 * max(1.0, hi):
            break

    if best_f >= M:
        return None
    return (float(best_f), float(best_t), s, d)


def apply_pair_move(a, s, d, t):
    """Apply a mass move and recompute convolution exactly."""
    t = float(t)
    if t <= 0.0:
        return None
    a2 = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    s = int(s)
    d = int(d)
    if s >= a2.size or d >= a2.size or s == d:
        return None
    a2[s] -= t
    a2[d] += t
    a2 = np.maximum(a2, 0.0)
    ssum = float(a2.sum())
    if ssum <= 1e-300:
        return None
    a2 /= ssum
    c2 = fft_convolution(a2)
    m2 = float(c2.max())
    return a2, c2, m2


def perturb_sequence(a, rng, strength):
    """Multiplicative random perturbation, preserving non-negativity."""
    strength = float(strength)
    if strength <= 0.0:
        return a.copy()
    if strength >= 0.9:
        strength = 0.9
    r = rng.uniform(1.0 - strength, 1.0 + strength, a.size)
    b = a * r
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    return b / s


def coordinate_refine(a0, time_limit, start_time, max_no_improve=15,
                      pair_budget=24, evals=20, verbose=False):
    """Exact two-coordinate descent with active-peak subgradient guide."""
    a = norm_nonneg(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a.copy(), float("inf")
    c = fft_convolution(a)
    current_m = float(c.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = current_m
    rng = np.random.default_rng(2024)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    stall = 0
    iteration = 0
    half = max(1, n // 2)

    while time.monotonic() - t_start < time_limit and stall < int(max_no_improve):
        iteration += 1

        if time.monotonic() - t_start > time_limit:
            break

        grad, grad_idx = active_gradient(a, c, K=48)
        active_count = min(16, c.size)
        if active_count <= 0:
            active_idx = np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            active_idx = np.argpartition(c, c.size - active_count)[c.size - active_count:]

        order = np.argsort(grad)
        source_list = order[-min(6, n):][::-1] if n > 0 else order
        dest_list = order[:min(6, n)] if n > 0 else order

        items = []
        considered = 0
        pair_budget = int(pair_budget)
        for s in source_list:
            si = int(s)
            if a[si] <= 1e-300 or considered >= pair_budget:
                break
            for d in dest_list:
                di = int(d)
                if si == di or considered >= pair_budget:
                    continue
                considered += 1
                if active_direction_ok(a, c, si, di, active_idx, 1e-12):
                    r = pair_line_search(a, c, si, di, current_m, evals=evals)
                    if r is not None and r[0] < current_m - 1e-14:
                        items.append((float(r[0]), len(items), float(r[1]), si, di))

        if not items:
            low_half = order[:half]
            high_half = order[half:]
            low_size = int(low_half.size)
            high_size = int(high_half.size)
            if low_size > 0 and high_size > 0:
                for _ in range(min(12, pair_budget)):
                    if time.monotonic() - t_start > time_limit:
                        break
                    si = int(high_half[int(rng.integers(high_size))])
                    di = int(low_half[int(rng.integers(low_size))])
                    if si == di or a[si] <= 1e-300:
                        continue
                    r = pair_line_search(a, c, si, di, current_m, evals=12)
                    if r is not None and r[0] < current_m - 1e-13:
                        items.append((float(r[0]), len(items), float(r[1]), si, di))

        accepted = False
        if items:
            items.sort()
            for item in items[:5]:
                if time.monotonic() - t_start > time_limit:
                    break
                _, _, tval, si, di = item
                if tval <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                res = apply_pair_move(a, si, di, tval)
                if res is not None:
                    trial, c2, m2 = res
                    if m2 < current_m - 1e-15:
                        a = trial
                        c = c2
                        current_m = float(m2)
                        if current_m < best_m - 1e-15:
                            best_a = a.copy()
                            best_m = current_m
                        accepted = True
                        stall = 0
                        if verbose and iteration % 20 == 0:
                            J = 2.0 * a.size * current_m
                            print("    COORD it=%d  J=%.8f  t=%.0fs" % (iteration, J, time.monotonic() - t_start))
                        break

        if accepted:
            continue

        stall += 1
        improved_random = False
        high_size = max(1, n - half)
        for _ in range(8):
            if time.monotonic() - t_start > time_limit:
                break
            si = int(order[half + int(rng.integers(high_size))])
            di = int(order[int(rng.integers(half))])
            if si == di or a[si] <= 1e-300:
                continue
            if grad[si] < grad[di]:
                tmp = si
                si = di
                di = tmp
            if a[si] <= 1e-300:
                continue
            r = pair_line_search(a, c, si, di, current_m, evals=10)
            if r is not None and r[0] < current_m - 1e-13:
                res = apply_pair_move(a, si, di, float(r[1]))
                if res is not None:
                    trial, c2, m2 = res
                    if m2 < current_m - 1e-15:
                        a = trial
                        c = c2
                        current_m = float(m2)
                        if current_m < best_m - 1e-15:
                            best_a = a.copy()
                            best_m = current_m
                        improved_random = True
                        break

        if improved_random:
            stall = 0
            continue

        if stall > 5:
            strength = 0.04 + 0.015 * float(min(stall, 8))
            a = perturb_sequence(best_a, rng, strength)
            c = fft_convolution(a)
            current_m = float(c.max())
            if current_m < best_m - 1e-15:
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_m = current_m
            stall = 0
            continue

        if verbose and iteration % 20 == 0:
            J = 2.0 * a.size * current_m
            print("    COORD stall=%d it=%d  J=%.8f  t=%.0fs" % (stall, iteration, J, time.monotonic() - t_start))

    return best_a, float(best_m)


def full_loss_grad(theta, n, tau, max_terms):
    """Smooth max objective and gradient for full-dimensional log-softmax parameter."""
    n = int(n)
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if theta.size != n or n == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(max(0, theta.size), dtype=np.float64)
    thc = np.clip(theta, -50.0, 50.0)
    f = thc - float(thc.max())
    e = np.exp(f)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = e / s

    c = fft_convolution(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    Mc = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(Mc) or Mc <= 0.0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    tau = float(tau)
    if not np.isfinite(tau) or tau <= 0.0:
        tau = 1e-12
    shifted = c / tau - Mc / tau
    shifted = np.minimum(shifted, 0.0)
    z = np.exp(shifted)
    sz = float(z.sum())
    if sz <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sz):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    loss = Mc + tau * float(np.log(sz))
    w = z / sz

    grad_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    K = max(1, int(max_terms))
    Lc = int(c.size)
    if K >= Lc:
        idx = np.arange(Lc, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(w, Lc - K)[Lc - K:]
    for qq in range(idx.size):
        ii = int(idx[qq])
        wk = float(w[ii])
        if wk < 1e-12:
            continue
        jlo = max(0, ii - n + 1)
        jhi = min(n - 1, ii)
        if jlo > jhi:
            continue
        offs = ii - np.arange(jlo, jhi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        grad_a[jlo:jhi + 1] += 2.0 * wk * a[offs]

    v = grad_a * a
    total = float(v.sum())
    grad = v - total * a
    mask = (theta > -50.0) & (theta < 50.0)
    grad = grad * mask
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return float(loss), grad.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def full_lbfgs_refine(a0, max_iter1, max_iter2):
    """Full-dimensional log-softmax L-BFGS refinement."""
    a = norm_nonneg(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a.copy(), float("inf")
    floor = 1e-12 / float(n)
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    a = norm_nonneg(a)
    theta = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = local_score(a)
    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return best_a, float(best_sc)

    theta_best = theta.copy()
    stages = ((0.10, int(max_iter1)), (0.02, int(max_iter2)))
    for factor, iters in stages:
        M = local_M(best_a)
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break
        tau = max(1e-12, M * float(factor))
        args = (n, tau, 160)
        try:
            res = sco.minimize(
                full_loss_grad,
                theta_best,
                args=args,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options={"maxiter": int(iters), "maxfun": int(max(2, 2 * iters)), "ftol": 1e-14, "gtol": 1e-10},
            )
            if res is not None and res.x is not None:
                th = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                th = np.where(np.isfinite(th), th, 0.0)
                th = np.clip(th, -50.0, 50.0)
                cand = param_a(th, np.eye(n, dtype=np.float64))
                sc = local_score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    best_a = cand.copy()
                    theta_best = th.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best_a, float(best_sc)


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    """Top-level search entry point. Returns the best sequence found."""
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = 1755.0
    try:
        bs = float(budget_s)
        if np.isfinite(bs) and bs > 90.0:
            total_budget = max(60.0, min(bs - 45.0, 1755.0))
    except Exception:
        pass

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    parent = None

    try:
        h = height_sequence_1
        harr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
        harr = np.ravel(harr)
        harr = np.where(np.isfinite(harr), harr, 0.0)
        harr = np.clip(harr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if harr.size > 0 and harr.sum() > 1e-100:
            parent = harr.copy()
            best_seq, best_score = try_update(None, float("inf"), parent, "parent", t_start)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = make_power_ramp(4096, 0.45)
        best_score = local_score(best_seq)
        print("  Fallback parent: score=%.8f n=%d" % (best_score, best_seq.size))

    if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size > 20000:
        best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, interpolate_to_n(best_seq, 12288), "parent_cap", t_start)

    if parent is not None and parent.size <= 20000:
        base_shape = parent
    else:
        base_shape = best_seq

    # Quick length sweep.
    quick_ns = {4096, 5120, 6144, 8192, 10240, 12288}
    quick_ns.add(int(base_shape.size))
    quick_ns = {int(x) for x in quick_ns if 2500 <= x <= 12288}
    for nq in sorted(quick_ns):
        if time.monotonic() - t_start > total_budget - 1400.0:
            break
        if nq == int(best_seq.size):
            continue
        cand = interpolate_to_n(base_shape, nq)
        best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, cand, "quick_n%d" % nq, t_start)

    base_n = int(best_seq.size)
    print("Base after quick sweep: n=%d score=%.8f" % (base_n, best_score))

    # Phase 1: LP / Frank-Wolfe direction at reduced length.
    rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    t_lp = min(rem * 0.10, 100.0)
    if t_lp > 25.0:
        n_lp = int(min(1024, max(256, max(1, base_n // 4))))
        a_lp = lp_search(best_seq, t_lp, time.monotonic(), n_lp=n_lp, n_iters=5)
        cand = interpolate_to_n(a_lp, base_n)
        best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, cand, "lp_n%d" % n_lp, t_start)

    # Phase 2: Exact coordinate refinement.
    rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    t_c1 = min(rem * 0.18, 300.0)
    if t_c1 > 30.0:
        if base_n > 10000:
            a_c, _ = coordinate_refine(best_seq, t_c1, time.monotonic(),
                                       max_no_improve=8, pair_budget=14, evals=16, verbose=True)
        else:
            a_c, _ = coordinate_refine(best_seq, t_c1, time.monotonic(),
                                       max_no_improve=14, pair_budget=24, evals=20, verbose=True)
        best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, a_c, "coord1_n%d" % base_n, t_start)
        base_n = int(best_seq.size)

    # Phase 3: Full-dimensional L-BFGS, if length is manageable.
    rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if base_n <= 6000 and rem > 120.0 and HAVE_SCIPY:
        if base_n <= 3500:
            it1, it2 = 22, 30
        elif base_n <= 5000:
            it1, it2 = 16, 24
        else:
            it1, it2 = 12, 18
        a_f, _ = full_lbfgs_refine(best_seq, it1, it2)
        best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, a_f, "full_lbfgs_n%d" % base_n, t_start)
        base_n = int(best_seq.size)

    # Phase 4: Parametric global/diverse search.
    rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if rem > 180.0:
        n_param = int(min(8192, max(512, base_n)))
        init_list = []
        if parent is not None and parent.size <= 20000:
            init_list.append(("parent", interpolate_to_n(parent, n_param)))
        init_list.append(("best", interpolate_to_n(best_seq, n_param)))
        init_list.append(("rampA", make_power_ramp(n_param, 0.35)))
        init_list.append(("rampB", make_power_ramp(n_param, 0.60)))

        runs = 0
        max_runs = 6
        for label, init in init_list:
            if runs >= max_runs:
                break
            if time.monotonic() - t_start > total_budget - 600.0:
                break
            for kind in (0, 1):
                if runs >= max_runs:
                    break
                if time.monotonic() - t_start > total_budget - 500.0:
                    break
                if n_param > 7000:
                    it1, it2 = 12, 16
                elif n_param > 4500:
                    it1, it2 = 16, 22
                else:
                    it1, it2 = 22, 28
                a_p = parametric_run(n_param, init, kind, it1, it2)
                cand = interpolate_to_n(a_p, base_n)
                best_seq, best_score = try_update(
                    best_seq, best_score, cand,
                    "param_%s_k%d_n%d" % (label, kind, n_param),
                    t_start
                )
                runs += 1

        rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
        if rem > 80.0 and HAVE_SCIPY:
            n_de = int(min(1024, max(256, base_n // 4)))
            a_de = de_param_search(n_de, interpolate_to_n(best_seq, n_de), 1,
                                   maxiter=35, popsize=15, seed=99)
            cand = interpolate_to_n(a_de, base_n)
            best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, cand, "de_n%d" % n_de, t_start)

    # Phase 5: Another strong coordinate refinement.
    rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    t_c2 = min(rem * 0.35, 400.0)
    if t_c2 > 30.0:
        if base_n > 10000:
            a_c, _ = coordinate_refine(best_seq, t_c2, time.monotonic(),
                                       max_no_improve=10, pair_budget=16, evals=18, verbose=True)
        else:
            a_c, _ = coordinate_refine(best_seq, t_c2, time.monotonic(),
                                       max_no_improve=16, pair_budget=28, evals=22, verbose=True)
        best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, a_c, "coord2_n%d" % base_n, t_start)
        base_n = int(best_seq.size)

    # Phase 6: Try larger lengths.
    rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if rem > 120.0:
        long_targets = (6144, 8192, 10240, 12288)
        for new_n in long_targets:
            if time.monotonic() - t_start > total_budget - 80.0:
                break
            new_n = int(new_n)
            if new_n <= int(best_seq.size) or new_n > 12288:
                continue
            cand = interpolate_to_n(best_seq, new_n)
            best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, cand, "long_n%d" % new_n, t_start)
            cur_n = int(best_seq.size)
            rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
            if rem > 50.0:
                t_short = min(rem * 0.15, 80.0)
                if t_short > 20.0:
                    if cur_n > 10000:
                        a_c, _ = coordinate_refine(best_seq, t_short, time.monotonic(),
                                                   max_no_improve=5, pair_budget=10, evals=14, verbose=True)
                    else:
                        a_c, _ = coordinate_refine(best_seq, t_short, time.monotonic(),
                                                   max_no_improve=8, pair_budget=14, evals=16, verbose=True)
                    best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, a_c, "long_coord_n%d" % cur_n, t_start)

    # Final polish.
    rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if rem > 40.0:
        t_fin = min(rem - 15.0, 250.0)
        if t_fin > 20.0:
            n_fin = int(best_seq.size)
            if n_fin > 10000:
                a_c, _ = coordinate_refine(best_seq, t_fin, time.monotonic(),
                                           max_no_improve=8, pair_budget=14, evals=16, verbose=True)
            else:
                a_c, _ = coordinate_refine(best_seq, t_fin, time.monotonic(),
                                           max_no_improve=16, pair_budget=28, evals=22, verbose=True)
            best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, a_c, "final_coord_n%d" % n_fin, t_start)

    if best_seq is None or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_seq = make_power_ramp(4096, 0.45)
        best_score = local_score(best_seq)

    final_a = norm_nonneg(best_seq)
    if final_a.size == 0:
        final_a = make_power_ramp(4096, 0.45)

    final_a = np.where(np.isfinite(final_a), final_a, 0.0)
    np.clip(final_a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=final_a)
    s = float(final_a.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        final_a /= s

    local_final = local_score(final_a)
    try:
        eval_final = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final_a])
        if np.isfinite(eval_final) and float(eval_final) < local_final:
            local_final = float(eval_final)
    except Exception:
        pass

    print("FINAL: score=%.8f  n=%d  t=%.0fs" % (local_final, final_a.size, time.monotonic() - t_start))
    return [float(x) for x in final_a]
```
